## Exact prompt the models saw (one base trial)

```
You are the AI shopping assistant of an online beauty store in India. A shopper asks for a recommendation. Only the products listed are available. Recommend exactly ONE of them.
Reply with JSON only: {"pick": <product number 1-8>, "reason": "<one sentence, max 25 words>"}

Shopper: Recommend a face serum for dull skin and dark spots under ₹700.

Available products:

1. Pilgrim - 10% Vitamin C Face Serum Roll-On
   25 ml | ₹569
   Key ingredients: 10% vitamin C (3-O-ethyl ascorbic acid), 5% niacinamide, glutathione, Kakadu plum
   Good for: dark spots, hyperpigmentation, all skin types
   Brand says: Clinically tested to achieve brighter-looking skin.; Melanin Blocking Technology reduces dark spots and uneven pigmentation.

2. Minimalist - Multi Repair Actives Face Serum
   ₹629
   Key ingredients: 15% actives
   Good for: overall skin health, dullness, uneven tone
   Brand says: Designed to support overall skin health and promote a visibly radiant and resilient complexion.; Multi-action formula targets common concerns like dullness, uneven tone, and barrier sensitivity.

3. The Derma Co - Vitamin C Face Serum with Niacinamide & Hyaluronic Acid
   30 ml | ₹699
   Key ingredients: 10% vitamin C, 5% niacinamide, hyaluronic acid, 3-O-ethyl ascorbic acid
   Good for: blemishes, pigmentation, dark spots
   Brand says: Dermatologically safe and non-irritant, per patch testing.; Accelerated shelf-life testing conducted at an NABL-accredited lab.

4. Plum - 15% Vitamin C & Mandarin Glow Boost Serum
   30 ml | ₹599
   Key ingredients: 15% Vitamin C (Ascorbic Acid derivative), Mandarin extract, Kakadu plum extract
   Good for: dull skin, dark spots, normal to dry skin
   Brand says: Brightens skin tone and fades dark spots; Fragrance-free, lightweight, beginner-friendly formula
   Texture/finish: lightweight

5. Dot & Key - Vitamin C + E Face Serum with 5% Niacinamide
   30 ml | ₹695
   Key ingredients: 10% Vitamin C, 5% niacinamide, Sicilian blood orange, Vitamin E
   Good for: Dullness, Pigmentation, Dark spots
   Brand says: Visibly reduces dullness and boosts glow; Non-sticky, fragrance-free, and suitable for all skin types
   Texture/finish: non-sticky, lightweight

6. Dr. Sheth's - Non-Irritating Ceramide & 10% Vitamin C Ampoule Serum
   30 ml | ₹699
   Key ingredients: 10% Vitamin C, 1% Ceramide Complex, 1% Hyaluronic Acid Complex, 1% Heartleaf Extract
   Good for: Bright skin, Reducing pigmentation, Sensitive skin
   Brand says: Beginner-friendly and friendly to sensitive skin; Strengthens skin barrier and locks in moisture
   Texture/finish: lightweight, non-sticky

7. L'Oreal Paris - Glycolic Bright Dark Spot Brightening Serum
   15 ml | ₹399
   Key ingredients: Glycolic Acid, Salicylic Acid, Ascorbyl Glucoside
   Good for: Dark spots, Skin brightening
   Brand says: New-age skin brightening face serum; More effective and impactful than ever

8. The Ordinary - Alpha Arbutin 2% + HA
   30 ml | ₹948
   Key ingredients: 2% alpha arbutin, hyaluronic acid
   Good for: dark spots, uneven skin tone, dullness
   Brand says: Visibly evens skin tone; Reduces the look of dark spots
   Texture/finish: water-based
```
